Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any bayesian_statistics module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 880. The projective resolution has length at most 623 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 880 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the bayesian_statistics structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 623. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
